#!/bin/bash

# Команда chcp есть в Windows, но отсутствует в Linux.
if command -v chcp >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    chcp 65001
fi

# Создание базы данных из SQL-файла лабораторной работы 2.
sqlite3 movies_rating.db < db_init.sql

echo "1. Для каждого фильма вывести название, год выпуска и средний рейтинг. Добавить ранг по убыванию среднего рейтинга. Одинаковые рейтинги получают одинаковый ранг. Вывести 10 фильмов с лучшими средними рейтингами."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "WITH movie_averages AS (
    SELECT m.id, m.title, m.year, AVG(r.rating) AS avg_rating
    FROM movies AS m
    JOIN ratings AS r ON r.movie_id = m.id
    GROUP BY m.id, m.title, m.year
),
ranked_movies AS (
    SELECT title, year, avg_rating,
           RANK() OVER (ORDER BY avg_rating DESC) AS rank_by_avg_rating
    FROM movie_averages
)
SELECT title, year, ROUND(avg_rating, 3) AS avg_rating,
       rank_by_avg_rating
FROM ranked_movies
ORDER BY rank_by_avg_rating, title
LIMIT 10;"
echo " "

echo "2. С помощью рекурсивного CTE выделить все жанры фильмов. Для каждого жанра рассчитать средний рейтинг фильмов и ранг жанра по убыванию среднего рейтинга с помощью RANK()."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "WITH RECURSIVE genre_parts(movie_id, rest, genre) AS (
    SELECT id, genres || '|', ''
    FROM movies
    WHERE genres IS NOT NULL
      AND genres <> ''
      AND genres <> '(no genres listed)'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT movie_id,
           substr(rest, instr(rest, '|') + 1),
           substr(rest, 1, instr(rest, '|') - 1)
    FROM genre_parts
    WHERE rest <> ''
),
movie_genres AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT movie_id, genre
    FROM genre_parts
    WHERE genre <> ''
),
genre_averages AS (
    SELECT mg.genre, AVG(r.rating) AS avg_rating
    FROM movie_genres AS mg
    LEFT JOIN ratings AS r ON r.movie_id = mg.movie_id
    GROUP BY mg.genre
)
SELECT genre,
       ROUND(avg_rating, 3) AS avg_rating,
       RANK() OVER (ORDER BY avg_rating DESC) AS genre_rank
FROM genre_averages
ORDER BY genre_rank, genre;"
echo " "

echo "3. Посчитать количество фильмов в каждом жанре. Вывести genre и movie_count, отсортировав по убыванию количества фильмов."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "WITH RECURSIVE genre_parts(movie_id, rest, genre) AS (
    SELECT id, genres || '|', ''
    FROM movies
    WHERE genres IS NOT NULL
      AND genres <> ''
      AND genres <> '(no genres listed)'
    UNION ALL
    SELECT movie_id,
           substr(rest, instr(rest, '|') + 1),
           substr(rest, 1, instr(rest, '|') - 1)
    FROM genre_parts
    WHERE rest <> ''
),
movie_genres AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT movie_id, genre
    FROM genre_parts
    WHERE genre <> ''
)
SELECT genre, COUNT(*) AS movie_count
FROM movie_genres
GROUP BY genre
ORDER BY movie_count DESC, genre;"
echo " "

echo "4. Найти жанры, в которых чаще всего оставляют теги. Вывести genre, tag_count и долю жанра в общем числе записей таблицы tags в процентах."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "WITH RECURSIVE genre_parts(movie_id, rest, genre) AS (
    SELECT id, genres || '|', ''
    FROM movies
    WHERE genres IS NOT NULL
      AND genres <> ''
      AND genres <> '(no genres listed)'
    UNION ALL
    SELECT movie_id,
           substr(rest, instr(rest, '|') + 1),
           substr(rest, 1, instr(rest, '|') - 1)
    FROM genre_parts
    WHERE rest <> ''
),
movie_genres AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT movie_id, genre
    FROM genre_parts
    WHERE genre <> ''
),
genre_tag_counts AS (
    SELECT mg.genre, COUNT(t.id) AS tag_count
    FROM movie_genres AS mg
    JOIN tags AS t ON t.movie_id = mg.movie_id
    GROUP BY mg.genre
)
SELECT genre, tag_count,
       ROUND(100.0 * tag_count /
             NULLIF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tags), 0), 2) AS tag_share
FROM genre_tag_counts
ORDER BY tag_count DESC, genre;"
echo " "

echo "5. Для каждого пользователя рассчитать количество оценок, средний рейтинг, дату первой и последней оценки. Отсортировать по убыванию количества оценок и вывести первые 10 строк."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "SELECT
    u.id AS user_id,
    COUNT(r.id) AS rating_count,
    ROUND(AVG(r.rating), 3) AS avg_rating,
    date(MIN(r.timestamp), 'unixepoch') AS first_rating_date,
    date(MAX(r.timestamp), 'unixepoch') AS last_rating_date
FROM users AS u
LEFT JOIN ratings AS r ON r.user_id = u.id
GROUP BY u.id
ORDER BY rating_count DESC, u.id
LIMIT 10;"
echo " "

echo "6. Сегментировать пользователей по поведению с помощью CASE: Комментаторы — тегов больше оценок; Оценщики — оценок больше тегов; Активные — и оценок, и тегов не менее 10; Пассивные — и оценок, и тегов меньше 5."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "WITH rating_counts AS (
    SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) AS rating_count
    FROM ratings
    GROUP BY user_id
),
tag_counts AS (
    SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) AS tag_count
    FROM tags
    GROUP BY user_id
),
user_activity AS (
    SELECT
        u.id AS user_id,
        COALESCE(rc.rating_count, 0) AS rating_count,
        COALESCE(tc.tag_count, 0) AS tag_count
    FROM users AS u
    LEFT JOIN rating_counts AS rc ON rc.user_id = u.id
    LEFT JOIN tag_counts AS tc ON tc.user_id = u.id
)
SELECT user_id, rating_count, tag_count,
       CASE
           WHEN rating_count >= 10 AND tag_count >= 10 THEN 'Активные'
           WHEN rating_count < 5 AND tag_count < 5 THEN 'Пассивные'
           WHEN tag_count > rating_count THEN 'Комментаторы'
           WHEN rating_count > tag_count THEN 'Оценщики'
           ELSE 'Сбалансированные'
       END AS behavior_category
FROM user_activity
ORDER BY user_id;"
echo " "

echo "7. Для каждого пользователя вывести имя и последний фильм, который он оценил, по времени ratings.timestamp. Пользователи без оценок также должны присутствовать, с NULL в полях фильма."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "WITH ranked_user_ratings AS (
    SELECT
        u.id AS user_id,
        u.name,
        m.title AS last_rated_movie_title,
        r.timestamp AS last_rating_timestamp,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
            PARTITION BY u.id
            ORDER BY r.timestamp DESC, r.id DESC
        ) AS row_num
    FROM users AS u
    LEFT JOIN ratings AS r ON r.user_id = u.id
    LEFT JOIN movies AS m ON m.id = r.movie_id
)
SELECT user_id, name, last_rated_movie_title, last_rating_timestamp
FROM ranked_user_ratings
WHERE row_num = 1
ORDER BY user_id;"
echo " "

echo "TASK05 ЗАВЕРШЁН"
--------------------------------------------------
Run: ./task5.bat
1. Для каждого фильма вывести название, год выпуска и средний рейтинг. Добавить ранг по убыванию среднего рейтинга. Одинаковые рейтинги получают одинаковый ранг. Вывести 10 фильмов с лучшими средними рейтингами.
--------------------------------------------------

    SELECT m.id, m.title, m.year, AVG(r.rating) AS avg_rating
    FROM movies AS m
    JOIN ratings AS r ON r.movie_id = m.id
    GROUP BY m.id, m.title, m.year

           RANK() OVER (ORDER BY avg_rating DESC) AS rank_by_avg_rating

       rank_by_avg_rating

5. Для каждого пользователя рассчитать количество оценок, средний рейтинг, дату первой и последней оценки. Отсортировать по убыванию количества оценок и вывести первые 10 строк.
--------------------------------------------------
SELECT
    u.id AS user_id,
    COUNT(r.id) AS rating_count,
    ROUND(AVG(r.rating), 3) AS avg_rating,
    date(MIN(r.timestamp), 'unixepoch') AS first_rating_date,
    date(MAX(r.timestamp), 'unixepoch') AS last_rating_date
FROM users AS u
LEFT JOIN ratings AS r ON r.user_id = u.id
GROUP BY u.id
ORDER BY rating_count DESC, u.id
LIMIT 10;

6. Сегментировать пользователей по поведению с помощью CASE: Комментаторы — тегов больше оценок; Оценщики — оценок больше тегов; Активные — и оценок, и тегов не менее 10; Пассивные — и оценок, и тегов меньше 5.
--------------------------------------------------
WITH rating_counts AS (
    SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) AS rating_count
    FROM ratings
    GROUP BY user_id
),
tag_counts AS (
    SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) AS tag_count
    FROM tags
    GROUP BY user_id
),
user_activity AS (
    SELECT
        u.id AS user_id,
        COALESCE(rc.rating_count, 0) AS rating_count,
        COALESCE(tc.tag_count, 0) AS tag_count
    FROM users AS u
    LEFT JOIN rating_counts AS rc ON rc.user_id = u.id
    LEFT JOIN tag_counts AS tc ON tc.user_id = u.id
)
SELECT user_id, rating_count, tag_count,
       CASE
           WHEN rating_count >= 10 AND tag_count >= 10 THEN 'Активные'
           WHEN rating_count < 5 AND tag_count < 5 THEN 'Пассивные'
           WHEN tag_count > rating_count THEN 'Комментаторы'
           WHEN rating_count > tag_count THEN 'Оценщики'
           ELSE 'Сбалансированные'
       END AS behavior_category
FROM user_activity
ORDER BY user_id;

7. Для каждого пользователя вывести имя и последний фильм, который он оценил, по времени ratings.timestamp. Пользователи без оценок также должны присутствовать, с NULL в полях фильма.
--------------------------------------------------
WITH ranked_user_ratings AS (
    SELECT
        u.id AS user_id,
        u.name,
        m.title AS last_rated_movie_title,
        r.timestamp AS last_rating_timestamp,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
            PARTITION BY u.id
            ORDER BY r.timestamp DESC, r.id DESC
        ) AS row_num
    FROM users AS u
    LEFT JOIN ratings AS r ON r.user_id = u.id
    LEFT JOIN movies AS m ON m.id = r.movie_id
)
SELECT user_id, name, last_rated_movie_title, last_rating_timestamp
FROM ranked_user_ratings
WHERE row_num = 1
ORDER BY user_id;

#!/bin/bash
chcp 65001

sqlite3 movies_rating.db < db_init.sql

echo "1. Название фильма, год выпуска, средний рейтинг и ранг фильма среди всех фильмов. Первые 10 фильмов."
echo --------------------------------------------------
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "WITH movie_ratings AS (SELECT m.id, m.title, m.year, AVG(r.rating) AS avg_rating FROM movies AS m LEFT JOIN ratings AS r ON r.movie_id = m.id GROUP BY m.id, m.title, m.year), ranked AS (SELECT title, year, avg_rating, RANK() OVER (ORDER BY avg_rating DESC) AS rank_by_avg_rating FROM movie_ratings) SELECT title, year, ROUND(avg_rating, 3) AS avg_rating, rank_by_avg_rating FROM ranked ORDER BY rank_by_avg_rating, title LIMIT 10;"
echo " "

echo "2. Все жанры фильмов, их средний рейтинг и ранг по убыванию среднего рейтинга. Используется рекурсивный CTE."
echo --------------------------------------------------
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "WITH RECURSIVE genre_split(movie_id, genre, rest) AS (SELECT id, '', genres || '|' FROM movies WHERE genres IS NOT NULL AND genres <> '' UNION ALL SELECT movie_id, substr(rest, 1, instr(rest, '|') - 1), substr(rest, instr(rest, '|') + 1) FROM genre_split WHERE rest <> '' AND instr(rest, '|') > 0), genre_ratings AS (SELECT gs.genre, AVG(r.rating) AS avg_rating FROM genre_split AS gs JOIN ratings AS r ON r.movie_id = gs.movie_id WHERE gs.genre <> '' AND gs.genre <> '(no genres listed)' GROUP BY gs.genre), ranked AS (SELECT genre, avg_rating, RANK() OVER (ORDER BY avg_rating DESC) AS genre_rank FROM genre_ratings) SELECT genre, ROUND(avg_rating, 3) AS avg_rating, genre_rank FROM ranked ORDER BY genre_rank, genre;"
echo " "

echo "3. Количество фильмов в каждом жанре. Сортировка по убыванию количества фильмов."
echo --------------------------------------------------
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "WITH RECURSIVE genre_split(movie_id, genre, rest) AS (SELECT id, '', genres || '|' FROM movies WHERE genres IS NOT NULL AND genres <> '' UNION ALL SELECT movie_id, substr(rest, 1, instr(rest, '|') - 1), substr(rest, instr(rest, '|') + 1) FROM genre_split WHERE rest <> '' AND instr(rest, '|') > 0) SELECT genre, COUNT(DISTINCT movie_id) AS movie_count FROM genre_split WHERE genre <> '' AND genre <> '(no genres listed)' GROUP BY genre ORDER BY movie_count DESC, genre;"
echo " "

echo "4. Количество тегов для каждого жанра и доля жанра в общем количестве тегов в процентах."
echo --------------------------------------------------
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "WITH RECURSIVE genre_split(movie_id, genre, rest) AS (SELECT id, '', genres || '|' FROM movies WHERE genres IS NOT NULL AND genres <> '' UNION ALL SELECT movie_id, substr(rest, 1, instr(rest, '|') - 1), substr(rest, instr(rest, '|') + 1) FROM genre_split WHERE rest <> '' AND instr(rest, '|') > 0), genre_movies AS (SELECT DISTINCT movie_id, genre FROM genre_split WHERE genre <> '' AND genre <> '(no genres listed)'), tag_counts AS (SELECT gm.genre, COUNT(t.id) AS tag_count FROM genre_movies AS gm LEFT JOIN tags AS t ON t.movie_id = gm.movie_id GROUP BY gm.genre) SELECT genre, tag_count, ROUND(100.0 * tag_count / (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tags), 2) AS tag_share FROM tag_counts ORDER BY tag_count DESC, genre;"
echo " "

echo "5. Статистика оценок каждого пользователя: количество, средний рейтинг, дата первой и последней оценки. Первые 10 пользователей."
echo --------------------------------------------------
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "SELECT u.id AS user_id, COUNT(r.id) AS rating_count, ROUND(AVG(r.rating), 3) AS avg_rating, date(MIN(r.timestamp), 'unixepoch') AS first_rating_date, date(MAX(r.timestamp), 'unixepoch') AS last_rating_date FROM users AS u LEFT JOIN ratings AS r ON r.user_id = u.id GROUP BY u.id ORDER BY rating_count DESC, u.id LIMIT 10;"
echo " "

echo "6. Сегментация пользователей по поведению: комментаторы, оценщики, активные и пассивные. Используется CASE."
echo --------------------------------------------------
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "WITH rating_counts AS (SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) AS rating_count FROM ratings GROUP BY user_id), tag_counts AS (SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) AS tag_count FROM tags GROUP BY user_id), user_activity AS (SELECT u.id AS user_id, COALESCE(r.rating_count, 0) AS rating_count, COALESCE(t.tag_count, 0) AS tag_count FROM users AS u LEFT JOIN rating_counts AS r ON r.user_id = u.id LEFT JOIN tag_counts AS t ON t.user_id = u.id) SELECT user_id, rating_count, tag_count, CASE WHEN rating_count >= 10 AND tag_count >= 10 THEN 'Активные' WHEN rating_count < 5 AND tag_count < 5 THEN 'Пассивные' WHEN tag_count > rating_count THEN 'Комментаторы' WHEN rating_count > tag_count THEN 'Оценщики' ELSE 'Сбалансированные' END AS behavior FROM user_activity ORDER BY user_id;"
echo " "

echo "7. Для каждого пользователя вывести имя и последний оцененный фильм, включая пользователей без оценок."
echo --------------------------------------------------

#!/bin/bash

sqlite3 movies_rating.db < db_init.sql

echo "1. Найти все пары пользователей, оценивших один и тот же фильм. Устранить дубликаты, проверить отсутствие пар с самими собой. Для каждой пары указать имена пользователей и название фильма. В списке оставить первые 100 записей."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"

sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "
SELECT DISTINCT
    u1.name AS user_1,
    u2.name AS user_2,
    m.title AS movie
FROM ratings AS r1
JOIN ratings AS r2
    ON r1.movie_id = r2.movie_id
   AND r1.user_id < r2.user_id
JOIN users AS u1 ON u1.id = r1.user_id
JOIN users AS u2 ON u2.id = r2.user_id
JOIN movies AS m ON m.id = r1.movie_id
ORDER BY m.title, u1.name, u2.name
LIMIT 100;
"
echo " "

echo "2. Найти 10 самых старых оценок от разных пользователей, вывести названия фильмов, имена пользователей, оценку, дату отзыва в формате ГГГГ-ММ-ДД."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"

sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "
WITH ranked_ratings AS (
    SELECT
        r.user_id,
        r.movie_id,
        r.rating,
        r.timestamp,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
            PARTITION BY r.user_id
            ORDER BY r.timestamp, r.id
        ) AS rn
    FROM ratings AS r
)
SELECT
    m.title AS movie,
    u.name AS user,
    rr.rating,
    date(rr.timestamp, 'unixepoch') AS rating_date
FROM ranked_ratings AS rr
JOIN movies AS m ON m.id = rr.movie_id
JOIN users AS u ON u.id = rr.user_id
WHERE rr.rn = 1
ORDER BY rr.timestamp
LIMIT 10;
"
echo " "

echo "3. Вывести в одном списке все фильмы с максимальным средним рейтингом и все фильмы с минимальным средним рейтингом. Общий список отсортировать по году выпуска и названию фильма. В зависимости от рейтинга в колонке Рекомендуем для фильмов должно быть написано Да или Нет."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"

sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "
WITH movie_avg AS (
    SELECT
        m.id,
        m.title,
        m.year,
        AVG(r.rating) AS avg_rating
    FROM movies AS m
    JOIN ratings AS r ON m.id = r.movie_id
    GROUP BY m.id, m.title, m.year
),
rating_limits AS (
    SELECT
        MAX(avg_rating) AS max_rating,
        MIN(avg_rating) AS min_rating
    FROM movie_avg
)
SELECT
    ma.title AS movie,
    ma.year,
    ROUND(ma.avg_rating, 3) AS avg_rating,
    CASE
        WHEN ma.avg_rating = rl.max_rating THEN 'Да'
        ELSE 'Нет'
    END AS 'Рекомендуем'
FROM movie_avg AS ma
CROSS JOIN rating_limits AS rl
WHERE ma.avg_rating = rl.max_rating
   OR ma.avg_rating = rl.min_rating
ORDER BY ma.year, ma.title;
"
echo " "
echo "4. Вычислить количество оценок и среднюю оценку, которую дали фильмам пользователи-мужчины в период с 2011 по 2014 год."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"

sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "
SELECT
    m.title AS movie,
    COUNT(r.id) AS ratings_count,
    ROUND(AVG(r.rating), 2) AS avg_rating
FROM ratings AS r
JOIN users AS u ON u.id = r.user_id
JOIN movies AS m ON m.id = r.movie_id
WHERE u.gender = 'male'
  AND date(r.timestamp, 'unixepoch') >= '2011-01-01'
  AND date(r.timestamp, 'unixepoch') < '2015-01-01'
GROUP BY m.id, m.title
ORDER BY m.title;
"
echo " "

echo "5. Составить список фильмов с указанием средней оценки и количества пользователей, которые их оценили. Полученный список отсортировать по году выпуска и названиям фильмов. В списке оставить первые 20 записей."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"

sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "
SELECT
    m.title AS movie,
    m.year AS year,
    ROUND(AVG(r.rating), 2) AS avg_rating,
    COUNT(DISTINCT r.user_id) AS users_count
FROM movies AS m
JOIN ratings AS r ON m.id = r.movie_id
GROUP BY m.id, m.title, m.year
ORDER BY m.year, m.title
LIMIT 20;
"
echo " "

echo "6. Определить самый распространённый жанр фильма и количество фильмов в этом жанре. Отдельную таблицу для жанров не использовать, жанры нужно извлекать из таблицы movies."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"

sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "
WITH RECURSIVE split_genres AS (
    SELECT
        id AS movie_id,
        '',
        genres || '|' AS rest
    FROM movies
    WHERE genres IS NOT NULL

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        movie_id,
        substr(rest, 1, instr(rest, '|') - 1),
        substr(rest, instr(rest, '|') + 1)
    FROM split_genres
    WHERE rest <> ''
),
genre_counts AS (
    SELECT
        genre,
        COUNT(DISTINCT movie_id) AS movies_count
    FROM (
        SELECT
            movie_id,
            substr(rest, 1, instr(rest, '|') - 1) AS genre
        FROM split_genres
        WHERE rest <> ''
    )
    WHERE genre <> ''
      AND genre <> '(no genres listed)'
    GROUP BY genre
)
SELECT
    genre,
    movies_count
FROM genre_counts
WHERE movies_count = (
    SELECT MAX(movies_count)
    FROM genre_counts
)
ORDER BY genre;
"
echo " "

echo "7. Вывести список из 10 последних зарегистрированных пользователей в формате Фамилия Имя | Дата регистрации (сначала фамилия, потом имя)."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"

sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "
SELECT
    substr(name, instr(name, ' ') + 1)
    || ' ' ||
    substr(name, 1, instr(name, ' ') - 1)
    || '|' ||
    register_date AS user_info
FROM users
ORDER BY register_date DESC, id DESC
LIMIT 10;
"
echo " "

echo "8. С помощью рекурсивного CTE определить, на какие дни недели приходился ваш день рождения в каждом году."
echo "--------------------------------------------------"

sqlite3 movies_rating.db -box -echo "
WITH RECURSIVE years(year) AS (
    SELECT 2006

    UNION ALL

    SELECT year + 1
    FROM years
    WHERE year < CAST(strftime('%Y', 'now') AS INTEGER)
)
SELECT
    year,
    date(year || '-08-11') AS birthday,
    CASE strftime('%w', date(year || '-08-11'))
        WHEN '0' THEN 'Воскресенье'
        WHEN '1' THEN 'Понедельник'
        WHEN '2' THEN 'Вторник'
        WHEN '3' THEN 'Среда'
        WHEN '4' THEN 'Четверг'
        WHEN '5' THEN 'Пятница'
        WHEN '6' THEN 'Суббота'
    END AS weekday
FROM years
ORDER BY year;
"
echo " "

2. Найти 10 самых старых оценок от разных пользователей, вывести названия фильмов, имена пользователей, оценку, дату отзыва в формате ГГГГ-ММ-ДД.
--------------------------------------------------

WITH ranked_ratings AS (
    SELECT
        r.user_id,
        r.movie_id,
        r.rating,
        r.timestamp,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
            PARTITION BY r.user_id
            ORDER BY r.timestamp, r.id
        ) AS rn
    FROM ratings AS r
)
SELECT
    m.title AS movie,
    u.name AS user,
    rr.rating,
    date(rr.timestamp, 'unixepoch') AS rating_date
FROM ranked_ratings AS rr
JOIN movies AS m ON m.id = rr.movie_id
JOIN users AS u ON u.id = rr.user_id
WHERE rr.rn = 1
ORDER BY rr.timestamp
LIMIT 10;

│ Dances with Wolves (1990) │ Elwin Turcotte   │ 4.0    │ 1996-06-27  │
│ Apollo 13 (1995)          │ Lyda Friesen     │ 3.0    │ 1996-07-09  │
└───────────────────────────┴──────────────────┴────────┴─────────────┘
 
3. Вывести в одном списке все фильмы с максимальным средним рейтингом и все фильмы с минимальным средним рейтингом. Общий список отсортировать по году выпуска и названию фильма. В зависимости от рейтинга в колонке Рекомендуем для фильмов должно быть написано Да или Нет.
--------------------------------------------------

WITH movie_avg AS (
    SELECT
        m.id,
        m.title,
        m.year,
        AVG(r.rating) AS avg_rating
    FROM movies AS m
    JOIN ratings AS r ON m.id = r.movie_id
    GROUP BY m.id, m.title, m.year
),
rating_limits AS (
    SELECT
        MAX(avg_rating) AS max_rating,
        MIN(avg_rating) AS min_rating
    FROM movie_avg
)
SELECT
    ma.title AS movie,
    ma.year,
    ROUND(ma.avg_rating, 3) AS avg_rating,
    CASE
        WHEN ma.avg_rating = rl.max_rating THEN 'Да'
        ELSE 'Нет'
    END AS 'Рекомендуем'
FROM movie_avg AS ma
CROSS JOIN rating_limits AS rl
WHERE ma.avg_rating = rl.max_rating
   OR ma.avg_rating = rl.min_rating
ORDER BY ma.year, ma.title;

4. Вычислить количество оценок и среднюю оценку, которую дали фильмам пользователи-мужчины в период с 2011 по 2014 год.
--------------------------------------------------

SELECT
    m.title AS movie,
    COUNT(r.id) AS ratings_count,
    ROUND(AVG(r.rating), 2) AS avg_rating
FROM ratings AS r
JOIN users AS u ON u.id = r.user_id
JOIN movies AS m ON m.id = r.movie_id
WHERE u.gender = 'male'
  AND date(r.timestamp, 'unixepoch') >= '2011-01-01'
  AND date(r.timestamp, 'unixepoch') < '2015-01-01'
GROUP BY m.id, m.title
ORDER BY m.title;

5. Составить список фильмов с указанием средней оценки и количества пользователей, которые их оценили. Полученный список отсортировать по году выпуска и названиям фильмов. В списке оставить первые 20 записей.
--------------------------------------------------

SELECT
    m.title AS movie,
    m.year AS year,
    ROUND(AVG(r.rating), 2) AS avg_rating,
    COUNT(DISTINCT r.user_id) AS users_count
FROM movies AS m
JOIN ratings AS r ON m.id = r.movie_id
GROUP BY m.id, m.title, m.year
ORDER BY m.year, m.title
LIMIT 20;

6. Определить самый распространённый жанр фильма и количество фильмов в этом жанре. Отдельную таблицу для жанров не использовать, жанры нужно извлекать из таблицы movies.
--------------------------------------------------

WITH RECURSIVE split_genres AS (
    SELECT
        id AS movie_id,
        '',
        genres || '|' AS rest
    FROM movies
    WHERE genres IS NOT NULL

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        movie_id,
        substr(rest, 1, instr(rest, '|') - 1),
        substr(rest, instr(rest, '|') + 1)
    FROM split_genres
    WHERE rest <> ''
),
genre_counts AS (
    SELECT
        genre,
        COUNT(DISTINCT movie_id) AS movies_count
    FROM (
        SELECT
            movie_id,
            substr(rest, 1, instr(rest, '|') - 1) AS genre
        FROM split_genres
        WHERE rest <> ''
    )
    WHERE genre <> ''
      AND genre <> '(no genres listed)'
    GROUP BY genre
)
SELECT
    genre,
    movies_count
FROM genre_counts
WHERE movies_count = (
    SELECT MAX(movies_count)
    FROM genre_counts
)
ORDER BY genre;

┌───────┬──────────────┐
│ genre │ movies_count │
├───────┼──────────────┤
│ Drama │ 4361         │
└───────┴──────────────┘
 
7. Вывести список из 10 последних зарегистрированных пользователей в формате Фамилия Имя | Дата регистрации (сначала фамилия, потом имя).
--------------------------------------------------

SELECT
    substr(name, instr(name, ' ') + 1)
    || ' ' ||
    substr(name, 1, instr(name, ' ') - 1)
    || '|' ||
    register_date AS user_info
FROM users
ORDER BY register_date DESC, id DESC
LIMIT 10;

┌──────────────────────────────┐
│          user_info           │
├──────────────────────────────┤

│ Reynolds Eda|2021-01-08      │
│ Herzog Filomena|2021-01-06   │

8. С помощью рекурсивного CTE определить, на какие дни недели приходился ваш день рождения в каждом году.
--------------------------------------------------

WITH RECURSIVE years(year) AS (
    SELECT 2006

    UNION ALL

    SELECT year + 1
    FROM years
    WHERE year < CAST(strftime('%Y', 'now') AS INTEGER)
)
SELECT
    year,
    date(year || '-08-11') AS birthday,
    CASE strftime('%w', date(year || '-08-11'))
        WHEN '0' THEN 'Воскресенье'
        WHEN '1' THEN 'Понедельник'
        WHEN '2' THEN 'Вторник'
        WHEN '3' THEN 'Среда'
        WHEN '4' THEN 'Четверг'
        WHEN '5' THEN 'Пятница'
        WHEN '6' THEN 'Суббота'
    END AS weekday
FROM years
ORDER BY year;

┌──────┬────────────┬─────────────┐
│ year │  birthday  │   weekday   │
├──────┼────────────┼─────────────┤
│ 2006 │ 2006-08-11 │ Пятница     │
│ 2007 │ 2007-08-11 │ Суббота     │
│ 2008 │ 2008-08-11 │ Понедельник │
│ 2009 │ 2009-08-11 │ Вторник     │
│ 2010 │ 2010-08-11 │ Среда       │
│ 2011 │ 2011-08-11 │ Четверг     │
│ 2012 │ 2012-08-11 │ Суббота     │

#!/bin/bash
set -e

# На Windows скрипт запускается через Git Bash.
if command -v chcp.com >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    chcp.com 65001
fi

cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -batch -bail < db_init.sql

# Общий CTE для запросов 2–4. Маркер отсутствия жанра пропускаем.
genre_cte="
WITH RECURSIVE split_genres(movie_id, genre, remainder) AS (
    SELECT id, '', genres || '|'
    FROM movies
    WHERE genres <> '(no genres listed)'
    UNION ALL
    SELECT movie_id,
           substr(remainder, 1, instr(remainder, '|') - 1),
           substr(remainder, instr(remainder, '|') + 1)
    FROM split_genres
    WHERE remainder <> ''
),
movie_genres AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT movie_id, genre
    FROM split_genres
    WHERE genre <> ''
)
"

echo "1. Десять фильмов с наивысшим средним рейтингом и их ранги"
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -batch -bail -box -echo -nullvalue NULL "
WITH movie_ratings AS (
    SELECT m.id, m.title, m.year, AVG(r.rating) AS avg_rating
    FROM movies AS m
    LEFT JOIN ratings AS r ON r.movie_id = m.id
    GROUP BY m.id, m.title, m.year
),
ranked_movies AS (
    SELECT id, title, year, avg_rating,
           RANK() OVER (ORDER BY avg_rating DESC) AS rank_by_avg_rating
    FROM movie_ratings
    WHERE avg_rating IS NOT NULL
)
SELECT title, year, avg_rating, rank_by_avg_rating
FROM ranked_movies
ORDER BY rank_by_avg_rating, id
LIMIT 10;
"
echo " "

echo "2. Средний рейтинг фильмов по жанрам и ранг каждого жанра"
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -batch -bail -box -echo -nullvalue NULL "${genre_cte},
movie_ratings AS (
    SELECT movie_id, AVG(rating) AS avg_rating
    FROM ratings
    GROUP BY movie_id
),
genre_ratings AS (
    SELECT mg.genre, AVG(mr.avg_rating) AS avg_rating
    FROM movie_genres AS mg
    LEFT JOIN movie_ratings AS mr ON mr.movie_id = mg.movie_id
    GROUP BY mg.genre
)
SELECT genre, avg_rating,
       RANK() OVER (ORDER BY avg_rating DESC) AS rank_by_avg_rating
FROM genre_ratings
ORDER BY rank_by_avg_rating, genre;
"
echo " "

echo "3. Количество фильмов в каждом жанре"
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -batch -bail -box -echo "${genre_cte}
SELECT genre, COUNT(*) AS movie_count
FROM movie_genres
GROUP BY genre
ORDER BY movie_count DESC, genre;
"
echo " "

echo "4. Количество тегов по жанрам и доля от всех записей таблицы tags, в процентах"
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -batch -bail -box -echo -nullvalue NULL "${genre_cte}
SELECT mg.genre, COUNT(t.id) AS tag_count,
       ROUND(100.0 * COUNT(t.id) / NULLIF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tags), 0), 2) AS tag_share
FROM movie_genres AS mg
LEFT JOIN tags AS t ON t.movie_id = mg.movie_id
GROUP BY mg.genre
ORDER BY tag_count DESC, mg.genre;
"
echo " "

echo "5. Десять пользователей с наибольшим числом оценок, их средние оценки и даты первой и последней оценки"
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -batch -bail -box -echo -nullvalue NULL "
SELECT u.id AS user_id,
       COUNT(r.id) AS rating_count,
       AVG(r.rating) AS avg_rating,
       date(MIN(r.timestamp), 'unixepoch') AS first_rating_date,
       date(MAX(r.timestamp), 'unixepoch') AS last_rating_date
FROM users AS u
LEFT JOIN ratings AS r ON r.user_id = u.id
GROUP BY u.id
ORDER BY rating_count DESC, user_id
LIMIT 10;
"
echo " "

echo "6. Число оценок и тегов каждого пользователя и его категория поведения"
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -batch -bail -box -echo "
WITH rating_counts AS (
    SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) AS rating_count
    FROM ratings
    GROUP BY user_id
),
tag_counts AS (
    SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) AS tag_count
    FROM tags
    GROUP BY user_id
),
user_activity AS (
    SELECT u.id AS user_id,
           COALESCE(r.rating_count, 0) AS rating_count,
           COALESCE(t.tag_count, 0) AS tag_count
    FROM users AS u
    LEFT JOIN rating_counts AS r ON r.user_id = u.id
    LEFT JOIN tag_counts AS t ON t.user_id = u.id
)
SELECT user_id, rating_count, tag_count,
       CASE
           WHEN tag_count > rating_count THEN 'Комментаторы'
           WHEN rating_count > tag_count THEN 'Оценщики'
           WHEN rating_count >= 10 AND tag_count >= 10 THEN 'Активные'
           WHEN rating_count < 5 AND tag_count < 5 THEN 'Пассивные'
           ELSE 'Без категории'
       END AS behavior_category
FROM user_activity
ORDER BY user_id;
"
echo " "

echo "7. Последний оценённый фильм каждого пользователя и время оценки"
echo "--------------------------------------------------"
sqlite3 movies_rating.db -batch -bail -box -echo -nullvalue NULL "
WITH ranked_ratings AS (
    SELECT user_id, movie_id, timestamp,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
               PARTITION BY user_id
               ORDER BY timestamp DESC, id DESC
           ) AS rating_position
    FROM ratings
)
SELECT u.id AS user_id, u.name,
       m.title AS last_rated_movie_title,
       r.timestamp AS last_rating_timestamp
FROM users AS u
LEFT JOIN ranked_ratings AS r ON r.user_id = u.id AND r.rating_position = 1
LEFT JOIN movies AS m ON m.id = r.movie_id
ORDER BY user_id;
"

WITH RECURSIVE split_genres(movie_id, genre, remainder) AS (
    SELECT id, '', genres || '|'
    FROM movies
    WHERE genres <> '(no genres listed)'
    UNION ALL
    SELECT movie_id,
           substr(remainder, 1, instr(remainder, '|') - 1),
           substr(remainder, instr(remainder, '|') + 1)
    FROM split_genres
    WHERE remainder <> ''
),

    SELECT DISTINCT movie_id, genre
    FROM split_genres
    WHERE genre <> ''
)

FROM movie_genres
GROUP BY genre
ORDER BY movie_count DESC, genre;

6. Число оценок и тегов каждого пользователя и его категория поведения
--------------------------------------------------

WITH rating_counts AS (
    SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) AS rating_count
    FROM ratings
    GROUP BY user_id
),
tag_counts AS (
    SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) AS tag_count
    FROM tags
    GROUP BY user_id
),
user_activity AS (
    SELECT u.id AS user_id,
           COALESCE(r.rating_count, 0) AS rating_count,
           COALESCE(t.tag_count, 0) AS tag_count
    FROM users AS u
    LEFT JOIN rating_counts AS r ON r.user_id = u.id
    LEFT JOIN tag_counts AS t ON t.user_id = u.id
)
SELECT user_id, rating_count, tag_count,
       CASE
           WHEN tag_count > rating_count THEN 'Комментаторы'
           WHEN rating_count > tag_count THEN 'Оценщики'
           WHEN rating_count >= 10 AND tag_count >= 10 THEN 'Активные'
           WHEN rating_count < 5 AND tag_count < 5 THEN 'Пассивные'
           ELSE 'Без категории'
       END AS behavior_category
FROM user_activity
ORDER BY user_id;

7. Последний оценённый фильм каждого пользователя и время оценки
--------------------------------------------------

WITH ranked_ratings AS (
    SELECT user_id, movie_id, timestamp,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
               PARTITION BY user_id
               ORDER BY timestamp DESC, id DESC
           ) AS rating_position
    FROM ratings
)
SELECT u.id AS user_id, u.name,
       m.title AS last_rated_movie_title,
       r.timestamp AS last_rating_timestamp
FROM users AS u
LEFT JOIN ranked_ratings AS r ON r.user_id = u.id AND r.rating_position = 1
LEFT JOIN movies AS m ON m.id = r.movie_id
ORDER BY user_id;

┌─────────┬────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────┐
